  Felice (di) Etterina Maria Nicola  124
  Fabrizio Verina ................ 160
Y después, directamente, la G. Nueve apellidos con F y ninguno es FAIENZA.

Translation

THERE IS NO RECORD TO TRANSCRIBE: this is a negative result, and it is filed as such.

The complete alphabetical index of the Paglieta birth register for 1899 was downloaded — the cover and all three pages of entries — and read letter by letter. The F occupies nine lines, all legible, and none of them says Faienza. Nor does the surname appear under any other letter of the index, or spelled any other way (Faenza, Fajenza).

The index leaves no gap: it is the register's official annual index, it covers records numbered 1 to 161 and also includes those of part II, marked «2ª P.». So at Paglieta, in 1899, no Faienza was born.

What you need to know about this record

  • FIRM NEGATIVE, AND IT IS HALF THE COMMISSION. In the Paglieta birth index of 1899 THERE IS NO FAIENZA AT ALL. Neither with the correct parents nor with any others: there is none.
  • THE VILLAGE WAS NEVER PROVEN. «Paglieta» came from the same family block, without a single citation, that had already been wrong twice over, both times verified. What turns a finding into proof is not the village: it is the parents. And the parents have appeared, but somewhere else.
  • WHERE HE MUST NOW BE LOOKED FOR: IN ATESSA. The marginal note on his mother's birth record says that Angiolina Pizzi married Nicola Faienza in 1892, record 101, IN THE REGISTER OF ATESSA. The maternal family has been in Atessa since 1730. The son's birth must be requested from the Nati of Atessa for 1899 and 1900.
  • AND THERE IS A REASON TO LOOK AT 1900 BEFORE 1899. The Argentine cemetery register gives Domingo Faienza as born on 1 MARCH 1900 and dead on 13 December 1985 aged 85, and the two figures tally with each other. If that date is good, his record is not in 1899 in any village. Paglieta 1900 does not exist in Antenati — zero records, verified — but ATESSA 1900 ought to exist: the Atessa series is one of the long ones.
  • A CURIOUS DETAIL FROM THE PAGLIETA INDEX: there is indeed a «PIZZI Bambino», record 142. A newborn with the surname Pizzi, with no forename in the index. He is not our Domenico — the surname is the mother's, not the father's — but it puts on record that the surname Pizzi also circulates in Paglieta.
  • COST OF THIS NEGATIVE: five images. That is what it costs to read a whole year at one go when the register carries an index at the end.

Translation

THERE IS NO RECORD TO TRANSCRIBE: this is another negative result, and it is filed as such. But it is a negative of a different kind from Paglieta's, and a good deal stronger.

At Paglieta the surname Faienza did not appear under any letter of the index and that was enough. At Atessa it appears four times, so it was not enough: all four records had to be opened and the parents checked. None of the four children is the child of Nicola Faienza and Angiolina Pizzi. They belong to three other Faienza families of the same village: that of MICHELE FAIENZA FU CARMINE and Celeste Serafini, in the contrada Piazzano, who had twins in December; and those of ANTONIO and VINCENZO FAIENZA DI PASQUALE — brothers, almost certainly, both of the contrada Carafella — each with one child that year.

Our Nicola is «figlio di GIUSEPPE». Not Michele fu Carmine, nor Antonio di Pasquale, nor Vincenzo di Pasquale.

So at Atessa, in 1899, no child of Nicola Faienza and Angiolina Pizzi was born, and certainly no Domenico Faienza of anybody.

What you need to know about this record

  • SECOND FIRM NEGATIVE, AND SOUNDER THAN THE FIRST. In Atessa 1899 there is no Domenico Faienza — between «Carmine» and «Luzio» is where he would go, and he is not there — and, moreover, none of the four Faienzas of that year belongs to our couple. It is not that the surname does not appear: it is that it appears and is ruled out one by one.
  • WHAT THIS ROUND HAS SETTLED IS THE VILLAGE. The marriage record of 1892 declares Nicola Faienza «nato in Atessa, residente in Atessa», and his father Giuseppe resident in Atessa. The family is from Atessa and not from Paglieta. What remains to be got right is not the place, it is the year.
  • AND THE YEAR THAT REMAINS IS 1900. With 1899 closed in both villages, the date from the Argentine cemetery register — 1 March 1900, consistent with the 85 years he is declared to be at his death in December 1985 — again becomes the good hypothesis. The search for «Atessa 1900» in Antenati returned zero records at the time; it is worth repeating, because the Atessa holding is one of the long ones and catalogue additions are frequent, and if it is still not there, to request the certificate from the Comune di Atessa.
  • A USEFUL MEASURE LEFT BY THIS SWEEP: the surname FAIENZA is common in Atessa. Three different families with children in a single year. That means that matching on the surname proves nothing, and that in the next round one must always look at the father's name: «di Giuseppe». It is exactly the same mistake that was made with Paglieta, only the other way round.
  • A DETAIL FROM THE SAME REGISTER: record 214, immediately above Faienza Luzio, is that of an Iacobitti Antonio with a marginal annotation from the consulate giving him as deceased in Carapachay (Buenos Aires) on 6 June 1957. The emigration from Atessa to the River Plate is written in the margins of these very books.
  • COST OF THIS NEGATIVE: twelve images for a year of 396 births. Four of the index and of the end of the volume, three of sampling and five of records.

Translation

Record number eight hundred and fifty-four. At SANTA FE, department of La Capital, on the twenty-seventh day of November nineteen hundred and sixty-seven, at eight fifteen. Before me, SANTIAGO SATALÉ, head of the Registry of Civil Status, there appeared don ALEJO RIESTRA, merchant, resident at Las Heras 3299,

declaring that at THREE THIRTY ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH of the present month, in this Capital, there died doña CRISTINA RIZZO DE SALERNO, of Argentine nationality, BORN IN THIS CITY, aged seventy-four, married, occupation housewife, Civic Registration number 1,529,760, as a consequence of illness, as stated in the medical certificate of Doctor OSCAR BENAZZOLA.

THAT SHE WAS MARRIED TO SETTINO SALERNO.

THAT THE DECEASED WAS THE DAUGHTER OF DON SALVADOR RIZZO AND DOÑA ÁNGELA COSTABILE.

This record having been read, it was signed with me by the informant and by don AURENCIO VILLAFAÑE, resident at Villa Yapeyú, competent witnesses.

What you need to know about this record

  • IT GIVES HER PARENTS, AND UNTIL TODAY THE RIZZOS WERE A SINGLE LOOSE PERSON IN THE WHOLE TREE. SALVADOR RIZZO and ÁNGELA COSTABILE. And it is not an isolated fact: the marriage record of 1923, forty-four years earlier and with a different declarant, gives exactly the same two names.
  • COSTABILE IS A SOUTHERN ITALIAN SURNAME, and it fits with the Salernos having come from RENDE, in Cosenza: the Calabrian colony of Santa Fe married among itself.
  • SHE DIED AT HALF PAST THREE IN THE MORNING. The registration was made three days later, and it agrees with the date on the municipal cemetery card.
  • HER YEAR OF BIRTH CANNOT BE FIXED WITH WHAT THERE IS. Here she declares 74 years (born about 1893); on her marriage record of 1923 she declared 23 (born about 1900). Seven years' difference. Her birth certificate would be needed, which according to this record is from the city of Santa Fe.
  • NEITHER THE DECLARANT NOR THE WITNESS WAS FAMILY — a shopkeeper from Las Heras and a neighbour from Villa Yapeyú. The same pattern as in the Faienza certificates: agents, probably from the undertaker's. Their addresses are NOT the family's.
Ancestor

Salvador Rizzo

Father of Cristina Rizzo. He entered the tree on 29 August 2026, through two Argentine certificates forty-four years apart that name him identically.

Married Ángela Costabile

Parent of Cristina Rizzo

Who this was
father of CRISTINA RIZZO, born in the city of Santa Fe between 1893 and 1900
Wife
ÁNGELA COSTABILE, Italian
Where he was
at Santa Fe: his daughter was born and married there
Sources
his daughter's marriage record (1923) and his daughter's death certificate (1967)

No record obtained yet

IT IS NOT KNOWN WHETHER HE WAS ITALIAN OR ALREADY ARGENTINE — his daughter's marriage record leaves the nationality gap all but illegible, and the death record does not say. CAN ONE WORK BACK FROM HIM IN THE ITALIAN ARCHIVES? YES, BUT ONE THING IS MISSING. On 29 August 2026 it was verified that RENDE is on the Antenati Portal **from 1809 to 1910, one hundred and one consecutive years**, with births, marriages and deaths. That is: if he and Ángela married in Rende around 1870-1890, the record is online and free to read, and it would name Cristina's four grandparents. THE PROBLEM IS THAT WE DO NOT KNOW WHICH TOWN THEY WERE FROM. Antenati is organised by comune: without a town no search is possible, because there is no global index of names. There are indications in favour of Rende —the surname RIZZO appears there in 1858, and his daughter married a Salerno of Rende— but they are indications, not proof. WHAT WOULD SETTLE IT AT A STROKE: the birth certificate of his daughter CRISTINA, born in Santa Fe city between 1893 and 1900. An Argentine record of that period names the parents with age, nationality and very often the town. It is on the list of requests to the Civil Registry. 29 AUGUST 2026 — SIXTEEN YEARS OF RENDE MARRIAGES HAVE BEEN READ AND THE RESULT GOES AGAINST HIM. The transcriptions of the Rende marriages from 1850 to 1865 were swept, year by year, looking for RIZZO grooms — because if he was born c. 1859, his parents married precisely within that stretch. In sixteen years there appear only THREE Rizzo grooms: Michele Maria and Pietro, brothers, both married in 1850 and sons of GABRIELE RIZZO and MARIA ESPOSITO FAVA; and Vincenzo, married in 1862, son of Michele Rizzo and Carmina Spina. And yet the surname is everywhere as the name of a MOTHER or of a BRIDE: Angela Maria Rizzo appears as mother at four different weddings, and there are besides Francesca, Eleonora, Arcangela and Beatrice Rizzo. It is exactly the same pattern that came out of the sweep of 1880-1885, where there were five Rizzo brides and not a single groom. That is: in Rende there are Rizzo women everywhere and very few Rizzo men who marry. For a man called Salvador Rizzo born there c. 1859 that leaves very few possible fathers. It does not rule him out, but it makes him considerably less probable than had been supposed. If Rende were the town even so, the only candidates for his father are the two brothers of 1850, and the check would be cheap: to look in the Rende births of 1856-1866 for a SALVATORE RIZZO son of Michele Maria or of Pietro. None of this enters the tree: they are another person's transcriptions, and there is not a single fact linking those Rizzo to him. AND THE SAME DAY IT WAS SEEN THAT THE SEARCH DID NOT HAVE TO BE CONFINED TO RENDE. The Calabrian transcriptions we had on disc were believed to stop in 1865, but that holds only for Rende: eight towns of the Cosenza ring run up to 1910 — Carolei, Cerisano, Falconara, Guardia Piemontese, the two Marano, Mendicino and San Vincenzo la Costa. That is to say that the years 1870-1893, which are the ones that matter for his wedding, can be swept in full and free of charge in eight neighbouring towns, and not only in the one that had been assumed. The first, SAN VINCENZO LA COSTA, has already been swept for six years —1880, 1881, 1882, 1885, 1886 and 1887—: not a single Rizzo and not a single Costabile, in a town whose repertoire of surnames is quite different from that of Rende. And the second, MARANO MARCHESATO, has been swept in full: nineteen consecutive years, from 1875 to 1893, and there too there is not a single Rizzo groom nor a single Costabile. That town was chosen because it is the one that has already yielded family of ours —the Volpintesta, the Palermo, the Luchetta—, so it was the most sensible setting. And out of the two sweeps something is beginning to emerge that is worth more than the result. Each of those towns has a repertoire of surnames very much its own that repeats year after year, and neither Rizzo nor Costabile figures in either of them. In Rende, by contrast, both are well documented: six Costabile households in a single generation and several Rizzo families. They look like surnames of Rende and of the city of Cosenza, not of the ring of towns. If that is confirmed in the six still to be done, the ring is not going to produce the wedding, and only two ways out remain: that it is in Rende between 1870 and 1893, or that the wedding was Argentine. AND BY SAMPLING THREE YEARS OF EACH OF THE SIX REMAINING TOWNS, ONE HAS TURNED UP. In MENDICINO, in 1885, there marries SALVATORE RIZZO, son of GIUSEPPE RIZZO and of ANTONIA FORTUNATA DODARO. He is the first Rizzo groom to appear outside Rende in the whole ring, and on top of that he is called Salvatore. He is not ours: he marries a MARIA ROSARIA FILIPPELLI, not Ángela Costabile. But he cannot be entirely ruled out — if that woman died young, a most ordinary thing, he could have been widowed, married a second time around 1888-1892, emigrated and had Cristina in Santa Fe. The dates fit without forcing anything. It can be checked free of charge and in the same place: by looking for Maria Rosaria Filippelli in the deaths of Mendicino for 1885-1893, and for a second marriage of his in the marriages of those years. If a Rizzo × Costabile wedding turned up in Mendicino, the search would be over. IT WAS CHECKED THE SAME DAY, AND IT DOES NOT COME OUT. Mendicino is now swept in full: nineteen years of marriages (1875-1893) and nine of deaths (1885-1893). In the marriages there are exactly TWO Rizzo grooms, and they are brothers —Antonio in 1883 and Salvatore in 1885, both sons of Giuseppe Rizzo and Antonia Fortunata Dodaro— and not a single Costabile in the nineteen years. And in the deaths, which carry a column for the spouse, no Maria Rosaria Filippelli married to a Salvatore Rizzo dies: the two who appear under that name are other women, married to a Piscitelli and to a Barone. That is: he was not widowed there, and the hypothesis of a second marriage is left without support. AND WITH THAT THE COSENZA RING IS CLOSED AND IT IS NEGATIVE. Ten stretches swept —Rende 1850-1865 and 1880-1885, Marano Marchesato and Mendicino in full, San Vincenzo la Costa, and samplings of Cerisano, Carolei, Falconara, Guardia Piemontese and Marano Principato— and in none of them is there a Rizzo × Costabile wedding. Putting that together with the fact that Ángela Costabile was not in the province of Santa Fe in June 1887 and that Cristina is born there between 1893 and 1900, the most probable conclusion, and now one with weight behind it, is that they married in Argentina between 1888 and 1900 and their record is in the Civil Registry of Santa Fe, not in Italy. All that remains is to read the years of Rende that are still unopened —1866-1879 and 1886-1893—, blocked for the last sixteen windows. And a warning that came up along the way, in Cerisano: there is there an ANGELA COSTABILE married to a SALVATORE — but a Salvatore MUTO, and of an earlier generation. Those two given names together with that surname are not a unique combination in the district: before claiming victory one must always look at the husband's surname. If in the eight of them no Rizzo × Costabile wedding were to appear between 1875 and 1893, that would be a very large negative: it would mean that they did not marry in the surroundings of Cosenza, and it would reinforce almost to the point of certainty that they married in Argentina between 1888 and 1900, which is where the census of 1887 and the scarcity of Rizzo men already point. Related: [[angela_costabile]], [[cristina_rizzo]].

COSTABILE is a southern Italian surname, and above all a Calabrian one. It fits with her daughter marrying a Salerno from RENDE, in the province of Cosenza: the Calabrian colony of Santa Fe married among itself

No record obtained yet

SHE IS ITALIAN AND WE DO NOT KNOW FROM WHERE, and that is the only door missing to go up this branch. WHAT HAS BEEN VERIFIED: COSTABILE IS A SURNAME OF RENDE, AND WITH NAMES ATTACHED. FEDERICO COSTABILE was the *assessore delegato* of the civil status of Rende in 1909 and signs the birth record of one of Settimo's sisters. SALVATORE COSTABILE, a tailor of twenty-three, was witness at a family wedding in 1897. And in the marriage index of Rende for 1858 there are the brothers SALVATORE and LUIGI GAETANO COSTABILE, sons of Francesco Saverio Costabile and Arcangela Maria Giraldi. That she was from Rende ceases to be a hunch based on the ending of the surname and becomes reasonable. BUT IT REMAINS UNPROVEN: Costabile is a surname spread throughout the province of Cosenza, and she could have come from any of its towns. WHAT WOULD SETTLE IT is the birth certificate of her daughter Cristina, in Santa Fe. 29 AUGUST 2026 — AND HERE THINGS IMPROVE FOR HER. The marriages of Rende from 1850 to 1865 were read in full, sixteen consecutive years. COSTABILE turns out to be there a large and old male lineage: eight Costabile grooms in that stretch, from SIX different households, all with father and mother documented — Diego Costabile with Carmela Infusino, Pasquale with Maria Rosaria Scaglione, Francesco Saverio with Arcangela Maria Giraldi, Giovanni with Rosaria Bartucci, Saverio Lorenzo with Elena Rosaria Porro, Domenico with Anna Maria Stillo and Natale with Costantina Leone. In 1857 a Costabile even marries a Costabile. That is to say: that a woman called Ángela Costabile should have come out of Rende fits well with what the town had. And the contrast with her husband is notable: in those same sixteen years there are eight Costabile grooms and only three Rizzo grooms. Nothing is proven yet —Costabile is a surname of the whole province of Cosenza— but of the two, the one who fits Rende better is her. A detail worth not forgetting: in the sweep NO Rizzo × Costabile wedding appears between 1850 and 1865. If that couple married in Rende, it was later. And if she reached Santa Fe after 1887 —which is what the census says— the wedding could perfectly well have been Argentine. Related: [[salvador_rizzo]], [[cristina_rizzo]].

Direct line

Carlos Francisco Salerno

Son of Settimo and Cristina, born in Argentina. A chartered accountant and a public figure in Santa Fe: president of Club Atlético Colón from 1970 to 1974 and an official in the provincial administration. He died on 13 August 2002. He is the only person in the tree with a published newspaper biography.

No record obtained yet

No record yet, but a published source, and he is the only one in the tree who has one. On 13 August 2002 EL LITORAL of Santa Fe devoted a whole obituary to him. WHAT THE NEWSPAPER PROVES. That he was an ACCOUNTANT. That he died «this early morning», that is, that of 13 August 2002 — the tree said 2003 from memory and stands corrected. That in 1970 he was elected vice-president of Colón on the «Nuevo Colón» list headed by José Relañez Passini; that at the end of that same year Relañez was removed and HE HIMSELF TOOK OVER THE PRESIDENCY, until 1974. That under his management the club came out of a difficult financial situation and the SWIMMING POOL, the CLUBHOUSE and the pit of the western sector were built, and the stadium was remodelled. That he obtained the money for the pool from the municipality and himself devised the legal form in which to receive it SO THAT IT COULD NOT BE DIVERTED TO FOOTBALL; the newspaper stresses «the seriousness and clarity with which he handled the finances». That through his hands passed Enzo Trossero, «Cococho» Álvarez, Trullet, Zuccarelli, Di Meola, Aráoz and Costantino, and the coach Urriolabeitia. And that he made a career in the provincial PUBLIC ADMINISTRATION, with some prominence during the second governorship of Sylvestre Begnis. THE ANECDOTE THAT PORTRAYS HIM BEST. On 25 January 1981 he stood again and the election was decided by FOUR VOTES. His list had been proclaimed the winner; it was HE HIMSELF who spotted the error that reversed the result, pointed it out and conceded defeat. The newspaper uses the word «hidalgamente». Years later José Néstor Vignatti called him the «spiritual father» of his group and consulted him constantly. WHAT THE FAMILY CONTRIBUTES AND THE NEWSPAPER DOES NOT SAY. That he was also president of the CHAMBER OF COMMERCE of Santa Fe and of EMAÚS. It fits that profile, but the obituary is from the sports section and looks only at Colón, so these stand here as FAMILY TESTIMONY and not as proven fact. They would be confirmed in the local press or in the archives of those two institutions. WHAT IS STILL MISSING. His BIRTH CERTIFICATE in the Civil Registry of Santa Fe, which would name Settimo under the name he used in Argentina and would give his declared age: it would be the first Argentine document to tie this branch to the 48 Italian records. And now also his DEATH RECORD which, with the exact date already in hand, is an easy request. Translation

At SANTA FE, department of La Capital, province of Santa Fe, on 21 August 1984. I, Public Officer of this Registry, record the DEATH of BRÍGIDA MARÍA ANA NELLI. Female, ARGENTINE nationality. Status: married. Occupation: housewife. Identity document: Civic Registration 6,119,349. Address: J. P. López 2643.

DAUGHTER OF ÁNGEL ALBERTO NELLI AND FILOMENA PAGLIARONE.

BORN IN THE PROVINCE OF SANTA FE ON 12 JANUARY 1901.

Occurring at Santa Fe on 19 August 1984 at 20:00. Cause of death: illness. Medical certificate Miguel Hominal. Informant: VÍCTOR BORREI, L.E. 8,501,792, address Belgrano 6578, acting by virtue of being a person authorised for that purpose. — María M. Pierozzi, Head of the Deaths Subsection of Santa Fe.

What you need to know about this record

  • WHAT PROVES IT IS HER, AND NOT SOME OTHER NELLI: THE ADDRESS. This record and that of DOMINGO FAIENZA give the same house — J. P. López 2643. She dies there in August 1984; he, in the same house, sixteen months later. They were husband and wife and lived together to the end.
  • MIND THE OTHER TWO ADDRESSES THAT APPEAR. Belgrano 6578 and Berutti 5663 are the addresses of the DECLARANTS, not of the family. Both records say that the declarant «acts by virtue of being a person authorised for that purpose»: they are agents, almost certainly from the undertaker's, and in each record it is a different man. Those addresses do not mean that there was another house or another family.
  • IT GIVES THE FATHER'S FULL NAME. The certificate of baptism said only «D. Ángel A. Nelli». It is now known that the A. is for ALBERTO. And with this record two people enter the tree who until today did not exist: her parents.
  • IT MATCHES THE CERTIFICATE OF BAPTISM IN FOUR THINGS: the full name in the same order (Brígida M. Ana), the month and the year, the father and the mother's name.
  • TWO POINTS OF FRICTION, noted without covering them up. (1) THE DAY: the baptismal certificate says 13 January, this record says 12. One day's difference between a paper written within days of the birth and another dictated 83 years later by someone who was not family. (2) THE MOTHER'S SURNAME: on the baptismal certificate I read PALLARONE; here the hand gives something like PAGLIQEOME. Neither reading is secure on its own, but together they point to PAGLIARONE.
  • And it confirms that she was Argentine by birth: «born in the province of Santa Fe». The certificate of baptism left that box blank.
Ancestor

Ángel Alberto Nelli

Brígida's father. He entered the tree on 28 August 2026, through his daughter's death certificate: until that day he was only an initial on a baptismal record.

Parent of Brigida Maria Ana Nelli

father of BRÍGIDA MARÍA ANA NELLI, born in the province of Santa Fe in January 1901
Full name
ÁNGEL ALBERTO. His daughter's certificate of baptism gave only «D. Ángel A. Nelli»; the death certificate of 1984 reveals the second initial
Wife
FILOMENA PAGLIARONE
Style of address
in the certificate of baptism of 1901 the priest gives him «DON», which at that time and in that book was not given to just anyone
Where he was in 1901
at Santa Fe city: his daughter was born and baptised there

No record obtained yet

IT IS NOT KNOWN WHETHER HE WAS ITALIAN OR ALREADY ARGENTINE, and that is the question to be settled. The surname NELLI is Italian, and his daughter was born in Santa Fe in 1901, at the height of the immigration. The shortest route would be his own death certificate in the Civil Registry of Santa Fe —which would give age, nationality and town— or the marriage record with Filomena. 29 AUGUST 2026 — WE NOW KNOW SOMETHING ABOUT HIM, AND IT IS BY ABSENCE. The 1887 provincial census of Santa Fe —which is free and indexed name by name— was swept in full looking for NELLI and PAGLIARONE under thirteen different spellings, because the indexer writes Italian surnames by ear (ALASIA appears in seven forms in that same census). PAGLIARONE does not exist in the whole province. Three people come up under NELLI, and all three fall away: two are boys of fourteen and thirteen inside a SWISS household —the Burgi, with the Pfeiffer next door— in HUMBOLDT, a Swiss-German colony in the department of Las Colonias; the third is a Juan of eighteen, also in Las Colonias. Our family always lived in the CAPITAL. THEREFORE ÁNGEL AND FILOMENA WERE NOT IN THE PROVINCE IN JUNE 1887. And since their daughter Brígida is born in Santa Fe in January 1901, they arrived between those two dates. That leaves their wedding within a bracket of fourteen years and in two possible places: Italy before embarking, or SANTA FE between 1888 and 1900. If it was the second, their record is in the Civil Registry of Santa Fe and would name Brígida's four grandparents, who today are four whole gaps. FOR THAT REASON BRÍGIDA'S BIRTH CERTIFICATE (January 1901) RISES IN PRIORITY: an Argentine record of that period gives the age and the nationality of both parents, and very often the number of years they had been in the country. It would answer once and for all whether he was Italian or already Argentine. Related: [[filomena_pagliarone]].

Ancestor

Filomena Pagliarone

Brígida's mother. Her forename is clear in two documents; the surname is the one weak point of this couple, because the two sources spell it differently.

Married Ángel Alberto Nelli

Parent of Brigida Maria Ana Nelli

Who this was
mother of BRÍGIDA MARÍA ANA NELLI
Husband
ÁNGEL ALBERTO NELLI
The forename
FILOMENA, beyond dispute: so both the certificate of baptism of 1901 and the death certificate of 1984 write it
The surname
PROBABLE, NOT PROVEN. In the certificate of baptism I read PALLARONE; in the death certificate the hand gives something like PAGLIQEOME. Neither reading is firm, but together they point to PAGLIARONE
Why it fits
PAGLIARONE is a real surname, and one from the ABRUZZO. Her son-in-law, Domingo Faienza, was from PAGLIETA, in the province of Chieti — Abruzzo as well. It is a suggestive coincidence, and nothing more than that

No record obtained yet

THE SURNAME IS UNSETTLED and it is the first thing to be put right about this couple. It would be resolved by the marriage record of Ángel and Filomena, or by Brígida's birth certificate in the Civil Registry of Santa Fe (1901), which would write it a third time. With three independent readings the surname would be fixed. AND THERE IS NO POINT IN LOOKING FOR HER IN ITALY YET. On 29 August 2026 the 1887 provincial census of Santa Fe was swept under eight spellings of the surname —PAGLIARONE, PALLARONE, PAGLIARON, PAGLIERONE, PALLIARONE, PAGLIARONI, PAJARONE and PAGLIARO— and there is NOT A SINGLE person of that name in the whole province. The only thing resembling it is a «Pagliaro Margarita» of forty-two in Las Colonias, which is a different surname. Together with the same absence of her husband, that proves that they arrived AFTER June 1887. What this means in practice: as long as the surname is not fixed and we do not know the town, looking for her in an Italian archive is throwing time away — Antenati is organised by comune and one cannot search a surname blindly across the whole of Italy. The correct order is the other way round: first the Argentine record that fixes the surname and gives the town, and then Italy. Related: [[angel_nelli]].

Ancestor

Ramón Marcos Rivas

In the records: RAMÓN MARCOS, not Marcos Ramón — so both his records and the cemetery register write it

Gonzalo's paternal grandfather. On 31 August 2026 he went from being the largest gap in the tree — a man with no dates, no village and no proven parents — to having TWO RECORDS from the Santa Fe civil registry. He was born at CAYASTACITO, department of SAN JUSTO, in November 1909, worked as a clerk at Calchaquí, married at Jobson in 1948 and died at Santa Fe in 2002, aged ninety-two. And his records name his parents at last: RAMÓN RIVAS, a landowner of San Justo, and ROSA BUDIÑO.

Child of Ramón Rivas · Rosa Budiño

Married Elva Agustina Celeri

Parent of Marcos Cesar Rivas · Héctor Omar Rivas · Beatriz Margarita Rivas · Raúl Rivas

Birth
28 NOVEMBER 1909, AT TWO IN THE MORNING, AT CAYASTACITO, department of SAN JUSTO, province of Santa Fe — in his mother's home. Act 66, registered on 12 December. SETTLED BY HIS OWN BIRTH RECORD on 3 September 2026
The date that kept shifting, settled
THE TWENTY-EIGHTH WINS. His 1948 marriage record said the 23rd; his 2002 death record and the cemetery register said the 28th. The birth record, made fourteen days after the birth, says TWENTY-EIGHT
How his father acknowledged him
ON 3 SEPTEMBER 1942, BY A NOTE IN THE MARGIN OF THIS VERY RECORD. «By act two hundred and one of the birth book of the San Justo office, Don RAMÓN RIVAS acknowledged the present registrant as his own natural son.» File 593. He was thirty-two; his father, fifty-three. In 1909 the father's box had been left BLANK
Both places are the same
the marriage says «San Justo» and the death certificate «Cayastacito». They do not contradict each other: Cayastacito is the LOCALITY and San Justo the DEPARTMENT. One gave the village, the other the district
Death
18 November 2002, at 07:35, in the city of Santa Fe — volume 5°B, record 1237, year 2002. Ten days before he would have turned ninety-three
Marriage
10 April 1948, JOBSON, department of Vera, Santa Fe — record 15, before Julio H. Deiber, head of the Registry of Civil Status
Father
RAMÓN RIVAS (14 January 1889 – 24 August 1971), Argentine, of SAN JUSTO, a HACENDADO. He was TWENTY when this son was born. He acknowledged him formally in 1942. His own death record, found on 3 September 2026, also gives his parents: Evergisto Rivas and Rita Álvarez
Mother
ROSA BUDIÑO, aged SEVENTEEN, single, a seamstress, unable to write. She went alone to the register office at Cayastacito to record him. Daughter of Antoliano Budiño and Teresa Álvarez. A new name in this tree, confirmed by all three records. By April 1948 she was already dead
Trade
EMPLEADO (clerk) in 1948; RETIRED when he died in 2002
Addresses
CALCHAQUÍ when he married, in 1948; and his house at Ruta n.º 1, kilometre 5.5 west, SAN JOSÉ DEL RINCÓN, where he was resident when he died. But he did not die there: the record says the death occurred in the CITY OF SANTA FE
Identity document
M 2,479,755
Spouse
ELVA AGUSTINA CELERI, whom he married at thirty-eight; she was twenty-six
Children
Marcos César (1950), Héctor Omar, Beatriz Margarita and Raúl
Siblings
at least one, who visited him in old age and looked just like him. Still without a name. One candidate was checked — a Francisco, natural son of a different Rosa Budiño — and ruled out: he died at three months old in 1913
Who reported his death
GUILLERMO BERTONE, document 24,232,601, resident at Santo Tomé, «by virtue of being a person authorised for that purpose» — that is, an agent, not a relative

Translation

Act number SIXTY-SIX.

At CAYASTACITO, department of SAN JUSTO, province of Santa Fe, on the TWELFTH day of DECEMBER nineteen hundred and nine, at ELEVEN in the morning. Before me, [V.] N. ÁVILA, registrar of Civil Status, there appeared DOÑA ROSA BUDIÑO, SEVENTEEN YEARS of age, SINGLE, of ARGENTINE nationality, by trade a SEAMSTRESS, resident of this village, declaring:

That AT HER HOME, on the TWENTY-EIGHTH day of NOVEMBER last, at TWO in the morning, was born a BOY of WHITE colour, who is the DECLARANT'S OWN NATURAL SON.

That he is the grandson, on the paternal side, of ———— (left BLANK), and on the maternal side of Don [Ignacio] ANTOLIANO BUDIÑO and Doña TERESA ÁLVAREZ.

And that the said boy has been given the name RAMÓN MARCOS.

All of which was witnessed by Don PASCUAL MAURIZI, TWENTY-NINE years of age, single, of ITALIAN nationality, resident in this village; and Don LUIS BOASSI, FIFTY-SIX years of age, married, of ITALIAN nationality, by trade a MERCHANT, resident in this village.

The record having been read, it was ratified in its content, signed together with me by the witnesses and by Don [S.] ROMÁN, of full age, of this neighbourhood, AT THE REQUEST OF THE DECLARANT BECAUSE SHE CANNOT WRITE.

════════ IN THE MARGIN ════════

«Directorate General […]. September THIRD, nineteen hundred and FORTY-TWO. By act TWO HUNDRED AND ONE of the birth book of the SAN JUSTO office of the current year, Don RAMÓN RIVAS ACKNOWLEDGED THE PRESENT REGISTRANT AS HIS OWN NATURAL SON. Be it recorded. File no. 593.»

(And above, another annotation: Santa Fe, 24 February 1972, a copy of this record is issued at the request of the interested party.)

What you need to know about this record

  • THIS IS THE RECORD THAT CLOSES THE RIVAS BRANCH. It was ordered to settle three things and it settled all three at once: the exact day of his birth, who his mother was, and whether his father acknowledged him. And it threw in the names of two great-great-grandparents nobody knew.
  • THE DAY IS THE TWENTY-EIGHTH. There was a five-day discrepancy that had stood unresolved for months: the 1948 marriage record said 23 November; the 2002 death record and the cemetery register said the 28th. Two against one, but the one had been declared by the man himself at thirty-eight. THE TWENTY-EIGHTH WINS, being what the register made fourteen days after the birth says. He was born at TWO IN THE MORNING, at home.
  • HIS MOTHER WAS SEVENTEEN, A SEAMSTRESS, AND COULD NOT WRITE. She went to the register office alone, unmarried, to record the child as her own natural son. The last line of the record says another person signed «at the request of the declarant because she cannot write». Gonzalo had feared she was fifteen; she was seventeen.
  • AND BY NAMING HER PARENTS, IT IDENTIFIES HER. The record states that the child is the grandson on the maternal side of ANTOLIANO BUDIÑO and TERESA ÁLVAREZ. That couple was already in this archive without anyone knowing it: they head the Budiño household of the 1895 national census, where a ROSA aged one appears. There were two candidate Rosa Budiños; this is the one.
  • THE FATHER'S BOX, BLANK. In December 1909 the child is registered with no father: the line for the paternal grandparents is left empty. Ramón Rivas was twenty and was not there.
  • BUT IN THE MARGIN, THIRTY-THREE YEARS LATER: THE ACKNOWLEDGEMENT. «September third, nineteen hundred and forty-two […] Don RAMÓN RIVAS acknowledged the present registrant as his own natural son.» By act 201 of the San Justo office, file 593. Ramón Marcos was thirty-two; his father, fifty-three. It is the documented answer to why he bore the surname Rivas.
  • SIX YEARS AFTER THAT ACKNOWLEDGEMENT he married at Jobson, and in the marriage record his father appears with a name and a trade. The order of things matters: acknowledgement first, wedding after.
  • AND THERE IS A THIRD ANNOTATION, top left: in FEBRUARY 1972 someone requested a copy of this record. Ramón Rivas had died in August 1971.
  • THE WITNESSES ARE TWO ITALIANS — Pascual Maurizi, twenty-nine, and Luis Boassi, a merchant of fifty-six — both residents of Cayastacito. Neither appears in the 1887 provincial census, so they arrived later: this is the settling of the colonies, which in those years was changing everything thereabouts.
Death

Death certificate

ciudad de Santa Fe, departamento La Capital, Argentina · tomo 5°B, acta 1237 · 18 de noviembre de 2002, a las 07:35

Certificado médico
Cristian Meser

Translation

ARGENTINE REPUBLIC, PROVINCE OF SANTA FE. Registry of Civil Status. Volume 5°B, record 1237, year 2002.

At SANTA FE, department of LA CAPITAL, province of Santa Fe, Argentine Republic, on 19 NOVEMBER 2002, I, Public Officer of this Registry, record the DEATH of RAMÓN MARCOS RIVAS.

Sex MALE, nationality ARGENTINE. Status: MARRIED. Occupation: RETIRED. Identity document: M 2,479,755. Address: RUTA N.º 1, KM 5.5 WEST, SAN JOSÉ DEL RINCÓN, Santa Fe.

SON OF RAMÓN RIVAS AND OF ROSA BUDIÑO.

BORN AT CAYASTACITO, SANTA FE, ON 28 NOVEMBER 1909.

Occurring at Santa Fe on 18 November 2002 at 07:35. Cause of death: illness. Medical certificate of CRISTIAN MESER. Informant: GUILLERMO BERTONE, identity document 24,232,601, address Santo Tomé, Santa Fe, acting by virtue of being a person authorised for that purpose.

What you need to know about this record

  • THIS RECORD GIVES THE VILLAGE, WHICH WAS WHAT WAS MISSING: CAYASTACITO. The cemetery register said where he was buried, not where he was born. With this it is now possible to request his birth record, which is the next link and the one that would name Rosa Budiño with her age and say whether Ramón Rivas acknowledged him.
  • AND IT FITS THE MARRIAGE RECORD RATHER THAN CONTRADICTING IT. There he said «San Justo»; here it says «Cayastacito». Cayastacito is the locality and San Justo the department, so both are true at different levels. One gave the village, the other the district. An archival curiosity: in the provincial census of 1887 Cayastacito still appears within the department of LA CAPITAL, not San Justo — the boundaries moved.
  • BUT THE DATE DOES CONTRADICT: here 28 November, on his marriage the 23rd. And here the informant is not the family but GUILLERMO BERTONE, «a person authorised for that purpose» — an agent. It is the same pattern as in the other four Argentine certificates in this archive: the informant is never a relative.
  • HE DIED TEN DAYS BEFORE HIS NINETY-THIRD BIRTHDAY, at twenty-five to eight in the morning. And the two addresses the record gives must not be confused: HE HAD A HOUSE at SAN JOSÉ DEL RINCÓN — Route 1, kilometre 5.5 — and that is where he was resident, but the death «occurring at Santa Fe» is in the CITY, not at the Rincón. And it confirms both his parents by a second route, fifty-four years after the marriage and with a different informant: RAMÓN RIVAS and ROSA BUDIÑO.
Ancestor

Elva Agustina Celeri

In the records: ELVA AGUSTINA on her marriage record — but she signs «Elba Celeri»

Gonzalo's paternal grandmother, one of at least five sisters. Until 31 August 2026 she appeared here as «Elba Celeri», with no dates: her marriage record gave her back her full name and the exact day she was born. Her father was Tebaldo Celeri, the immigrant from Trassilico; Tuscany enters the tree through her.

the record calls her ELVA AGUSTINA CELERI, but at the foot she herself signs «Elba Celeri», which is what the family called her. Both are hers; when searching an index, try both

No record obtained yet

Her marriage record, filed on Ramón Marcos Rivas's card, at last gave her a full name and a date: ELVA AGUSTINA CELERI, born at JOBSON on 26 DECEMBER 1921. WHAT THAT CONFIRMS. That she was the daughter of Tebaldo Celeri and Margarita Alasio, something only family memory had said until now. And that in April 1948 her father was already dead while her mother was living, at Jobson — the last known trace of Margarita. WHAT IS MISSING IS HER BIRTH RECORD, Jobson or the department of Vera, 26 December 1921. It would give the ages of Tebaldo and Margarita in 1921 and their exact address, and would be the first Argentine document to describe Tebaldo as an old man. And her DEATH, with no known date. AND HER FOUR SISTERS ARE STILL MISSING: Olga, Ercilia, Rosa and Nerina, all on family memory. With Elva's date it is now known where to look — the births of Jobson and Vera in the 1920s — and NERINA is the rarest name and therefore the easiest to find.

Marriage

Marriage of Ramón Marcos and Elva Agustina

They married on 10 April 1948 at JOBSON, department of Vera. He was thirty-eight and she was twenty-six. This is the record that opened the whole Rivas branch: until it turned up, not even the names of his parents were known. And it comes six years after his father formally acknowledged him.

Date and place
10 April 1948, JOBSON, department of Vera, province of Santa Fe — act 15, before Julio H. Bertolino
Him
RAMÓN MARCOS RIVAS, thirty-eight, an employee, resident at CALCHAQUÍ. Son of Ramón Rivas and of Rosa Budiño, already deceased
Her
ELVA AGUSTINA CELERI, twenty-six, an employee, of Jobson. Daughter of Tebaldo Celeri, already deceased, and of Margarita Alasio
Why it matters so much
THIS IS THE DOCUMENT THAT OPENED THE RIVAS BRANCH. Before it, Ramón Rivas and Rosa Budiño did not exist in this family even as a memory. From it came their names, his trade — hacendado — his residence at San Justo, and the fact that she was already dead
The four misspelled names
the record corrects both bride and groom: he is not «Marcos Ramón» but RAMÓN MARCOS, and she is not «Elba» but ELVA AGUSTINA — though at the foot she signs «Elba Celeri», which is what she was called. And it writes her mother as ALASIO instead of Alasia

Marriage

Marriage record with Elva Agustina Celeri

Jobson, departamento Vera, Santa Fe, Argentina · acta 15 · 10 de abril de 1948

Archive
Registro Civil de la Provincia de Santa Fe — Archivo General
Cómo se consiguió
pedida en línea por Gonzalo el 31 de agosto de 2026, expedida ese mismo día. Trámite 26-00-287-372
Registrar
Julio H. Deiber, jefe del Registro del Estado Civil de Jobson
Formulario
«Modelo B» — el impreso de matrimonios de la provincia

Translation

Record number FIFTEEN. At JOBSON, department of VERA, province of Santa Fe, on the TENTH day of APRIL in the year nineteen hundred and FORTY-EIGHT. Before me, JULIO H. DEIBER, head of the Registry of Civil Status, there appeared:

Don RAMÓN MARCOS RIVAS, of ARGENTINE nationality, UNMARRIED, BORN AT SAN JUSTO on the TWENTY-THIRD OF NOVEMBER NINETEEN HUNDRED AND NINE, resident at CALCHAQUÍ and by trade a CLERK; SON OF DON RAMÓN RIVAS, of ARGENTINE nationality, resident at SAN JUSTO and by trade a HACENDADO (landowner), and OF DOÑA ROSA BUDIÑO, DECEASED.

And doña ELVA AGUSTINA CELERI, of ARGENTINE nationality, UNMARRIED, BORN AT JOBSON on the TWENTY-SIXTH OF DECEMBER NINETEEN HUNDRED AND TWENTY-ONE, resident at JOBSON and by trade a CLERK; daughter of don TEBALDO CELERI, DECEASED, and of doña MARGARITA ALASIO, of ARGENTINE nationality, by trade «domésticos» and resident at JOBSON.

Both parties stated that it was their will to marry, taking each other as husband and wife, and that there was no impediment whatever to the celebration of their marriage.

The witnesses don ESTEBAN LUIS CORTI, aged THIRTY-FIVE, of Argentine nationality, resident at CALCHAQUÍ, and don EDUARDO CEPZ, aged FORTY-ONE, of Argentine nationality, resident at JOBSON, neighbours and competent for this act, declared as to the identity of the future spouses.

The articles 50, 51 and 53 of the national marriage law having been read, I asked don Ramón Marcos Rivas whether he wished doña Elva Agustina Celeri for his wife, and her whether she wished him for her husband; and both having answered yes, I declare that they are joined in lawful marriage. The record having been read, the parties expressed their agreement and SIGNED IT with the witnesses. — She signs «Elba Celeri».

What you need to know about this record

  • THIS IS THE RECORD THAT OPENS THE RIVAS BRANCH, THE ONLY ONE WITHOUT A SINGLE PAPER. Until 31 August 2026 this whole branch — thirty-two people — stood in the tree on family memory and not one document. This record names all four parents of the couple at once and gives dates and villages.
  • RAMÓN RIVAS EXISTS, AND HE WAS A LANDOWNER. His father's card said «Ramón in theory — the name comes from family tradition, not from a document». Now there is a document, and it says more than expected: ARGENTINE, RESIDENT AT SAN JUSTO and by trade a HACENDADO. He was no day labourer: in the Argentina of the time a hacendado is an owner of land. The death certificate of 2002 confirms it by a second route.
  • ROSA BUDIÑO IS A NEW NAME IN THIS TREE. Marcos Ramón's mother did not appear anywhere, not even as a memory. The record names her and adds one fact: by April 1948 SHE WAS ALREADY DEAD. The form puts «fallecida» in the box for her nationality, which is how these forms mark dead parents.
  • AND IT CORRECTS THE NAMES OF BOTH GRANDPARENTS. He is not «Marcos Ramón» but RAMÓN MARCOS: the record writes it so six times, and his death certificate and the cemetery register agree. And she is not «Elba Celeri» but ELVA AGUSTINA CELERI — although at the foot of the record she herself signs «Elba Celeri», which is what she was called. Both matter when ordering papers: anyone searching an alphabetical index for «Marcos Ramón» or «Elba» may not find them.
  • HIS DATE DOES NOT MATCH HIS DEATH CERTIFICATE. Here he declares he was born on the TWENTY-THIRD of November 1909; his death certificate of 2002 and the cemetery register say the TWENTY-EIGHTH. Five days apart. Two sources against one — but the one is himself, at thirty-eight, and the other two are declared by an agent fifty-four years later. IT CANNOT BE SETTLED WITHOUT HIS BIRTH RECORD.
  • TEBALDO CELERI WAS ALREADY DEAD by April 1948, and MARGARITA ALASIA WAS STILL ALIVE, resident at Jobson and by trade «domésticos». It is the last known trace of her. Her surname is written ALASIO here, one more spelling to add to the seven from the 1887 census.
  • WHERE THEY LIVED: he at CALCHAQUÍ, she at JOBSON — both in the north of Santa Fe, and Jobson is exactly where Tebaldo Celeri had settled on arriving from Trassilico. Both are recorded as CLERKS, not as farm people.
Ancestor

Rosa Budiño

Gonzalo's great-grandmother. She was not in this tree even as a memory, and for a few days she was a name with two possible candidates. Her son's birth record identified her beyond doubt: daughter of Antoliano Budiño and Teresa Álvarez, the one-year-old girl of the 1895 census. She was SEVENTEEN when she went alone to the register office at Cayastacito to record her son; she was a seamstress, she was single, and she could not write. She had died before April 1948.

Child of Antoliano Budiño · Teresa Álvarez

Married Ramón Rivas

Parent of Ramón Marcos Rivas

Confirmed by record
YES, and by three: her son's birth record (Cayastacito, 1909, act 66), his marriage (Jobson, 1948) and his death record (Santa Fe, 2002)
Birth
about 1892: she declares SEVENTEEN YEARS in December 1909. The 1895 census gives her one year old, which would mean 1894 — two years' difference, normal in declared ages. No exact date
Parents
ANTOLIANO BUDIÑO and TERESA ÁLVAREZ, according to her son's birth record, which names her as the child's maternal grandmother. They are the couple heading the Budiño household in the city of Santa Fe in 1895
Trade
SEAMSTRESS, at seventeen, at Cayastacito
She could not write
the last line of the record says so: it was signed for her by «Don [S.] Román, of full age, of this neighbourhood, AT THE REQUEST OF THE DECLARANT BECAUSE SHE CANNOT WRITE»
Marital status
SINGLE. She registered the child as HER OWN NATURAL SON, with the father's box left blank
Son
RAMÓN MARCOS RIVAS, born at Cayastacito on 28 November 1909 at two in the morning, in her own home
Her partner
RAMÓN RIVAS, a landowner of San Justo, who was twenty. THERE IS NO RECORD THAT THEY MARRIED, and he acknowledged the child thirty-three years later
Dead before
April 1948 — her son's marriage record marks her «deceased»
The surname
BUDIÑO is a GALICIAN surname, from Pontevedra and Ourense. But the Budiños of Santa Fe came from CÓRDOBA: her grandmother Luisa was born there about 1827
Her world, in figures
From the 1887 provincial census: of the people of Santa Fe enumerated, 87,042 COULD NOT WRITE and 62,608 could — 58% were illiterate. SEAMSTRESS was the SIXTH most common occupation in the province, with 2,689 women. And ESTANCIERO, her father's trade, the SEVENTH, with 2,460
How many Budiños there were
MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED. In the 1887 provincial census there are SEVENTEEN people of that surname in the whole province, ten of them at HELVECIA — and among those, her father
Source
her son's birth record, in his profile; and the 1887 and 1895 censuses, in her father's profile

No record obtained yet

IDENTIFIED, BUT WITHOUT A SINGLE PAPER OF HER OWN. Neither her birth nor her death survives, and there is no photograph. Everything known about her comes from other people's documents: her son's birth record, which gives her age, her trade, her status and her parents' names; and the two censuses, which place her as a child in Antoliano Budiño's house. What is missing is her death, before April 1948, which would give her exact age and date of birth. She is not in FamilySearch's «Argentina, Cemetery Records», where a search has already been made.

Ancestor

Evergisto Rivas

In the records: «Evargisto» in the 1887 census; «Evergisto» in his son's death record

Gonzalo's great-great-grandfather and the first Rivas in this genealogy with a name of his own. He was born in CÓRDOBA about 1853 and brought his family to Santa Fe around 1884. In the 1887 census he is head of household at HELVECIA, married to Rita Álvarez, and the dependency column reads PATRÓN — an employer. Two years later his son Ramón was born nearby, by then in San Justo.

Married Rita Álvarez

Parent of Ramón Rivas

Confirmed by record
YES, and by two sources that do not know of each other: his son Ramón's death record (San Justo, 1971, act 84) names him, and the 1887 provincial census has him with his whole household
Birth
about 1853 in CÓRDOBA — he was 34 in the 1887 census
Where they came from
FROM CÓRDOBA, NOT SANTA FE. He and his wife were born there, and so were their two elder daughters. The first child born in Santa Fe was 2 in 1887, so the family crossed about 1884 or 1885
Where he lived
HELVECIA, department of San José, RURAL population, book 9, line 7 of the 1887 roll. Helvecia is on the Paraná just south of Cayastá
Standing
HEAD of household and, in the dependency column, PATRÓN — that is, he gave work rather than took it. His son Ramón would be a HACENDADO
Sabía leer y escribir
NO, neither he nor his wife. The 1887 census asks it in two separate columns and both say «no» for both of them
Spouse
RITA ÁLVAREZ, to whom he had been married SEVEN YEARS in 1887, so since 1880
Known children
MARGARITA (about 1882, Córdoba) · DESIDERIA (about 1884, Córdoba) · EVERGISTO (about 1885, Santa Fe) · and RAMÓN, born 14 January 1889 at San Justo, too late for the census
Careful with the name
the census writes EVARGISTO and his son's death record EVERGISTO. Neither is «Evaristo», which is what one would expect and what will make an index search fail
Death
not located

The Rivas household at Helvecia, 1887 provincial census

Helvecia, departamento de San José, Santa Fe, Argentina · volume 3, book 9, lines 7 to 10 and the next · 1887

Archivo General de la Provincia de Santa Fe · CFI
Colección
Primer Censo Provincial de Santa Fe, 1887 — padrones con información nominativa, buscador público y gratuito
Cómo se encontró
reconstruido por números de ficha correlativos: en este censo el índice sigue el orden de las líneas del padrón, así que pidiendo las fichas contiguas sale el hogar entero
Enlace
santafe.gov.ar/censo1887 — fichas 73497 a 73501 (Rivas) y 73360 a 73367 (Budiño)

Translation

The roll's records, one per person, in the order in which they are written:

· line 7 · RIVAS EVARGISTO, male, 34, HEAD, married, Argentine, born in Córdoba. Dependency: PATRÓN (employer). · line 8 · ÁLVARES RITA, female, 25, WIFE, married, Argentine. Children borne: 3. Years married: 7. · line 9 · RIVAS MARGARITA, female, 5, daughter, born in Córdoba. · line 10 · RIVAS DESIDERIA, female, 3, daughter, born in Córdoba. · line 1 of the next book · RIVAS EVARGISTO, male, 2, son, born in SANTA FE.

Population: RURAL.

What you need to know about this record

  • ESTE HOGAR SE ENCONTRÓ PORQUE LA DEFUNCIÓN DE 1971 DIO LOS DOS NOMBRES. Sin «Evergisto Rivas y Rita Álvarez» no había manera de distinguirlo entre los sesenta y ocho Rivas de la provincia. Con los dos nombres juntos, y encima como jefe y esposa en líneas consecutivas, no hay duda posible.
  • LAS EDADES DE LOS HIJOS DATAN LA MUDANZA. Margarita (5) y Desideria (3) nacieron en CÓRDOBA; Evargisto hijo (2) ya nació en SANTA FE. La familia cruzó de una provincia a otra hacia 1884 o 1885, cuando él tenía unos treinta años.
  • «3 HIJOS, 7 AÑOS DE MATRIMONIO» CUADRA EXACTAMENTE con los tres niños listados, y sitúa la boda hacia 1880. Ramón, el bisabuelo de Gonzalo, es el cuarto y nació año y medio después de este censo.
  • Y RESUELVE LA RAREZA QUE HABÍA QUEDADO PENDIENTE. Se había medido que en 1887 no había NI UN SOLO Rivas en el departamento de San Justo, sobre 1.655 personas indexadas, y sin embargo Ramón nació allí en enero de 1889. La respuesta es que en 1887 la familia estaba en HELVECIA, en el departamento de al lado. Se movieron entre una fecha y otra.
  • NI ÉL NI SU MUJER SABÍAN LEER NI ESCRIBIR, y el censo lo pregunta en dos casillas separadas. En el conjunto de la provincia, 87.042 personas no sabían escribir y 62.608 sí.
  • CUÁNTO PESABAN LOS DOS HOGARES EN EL PUEBLO, PORQUE LA PREGUNTA VALE LA PENA. Helvecia tenía 2.439 censados en 1887 —el décimo distrito más poblado de la provincia—, y entre las dos casas, Rivas y Budiño, suman TRECE personas: el 0,5%, una de cada ciento noventa. No eran el pueblo, eran dos casas de un pueblo mediano.
  • Y SER DE CÓRDOBA ALLÍ NO ERA RARO: TAMBIÉN SE MIDIÓ. Sobre una muestra de cincuenta fichas correlativas de ese mismo tramo del padrón, OCHO habían nacido en Córdoba: el 16%. En el conjunto de la provincia los nacidos en Córdoba son 9.467 sobre 156.502 indexados, un 6%. Helvecia tenía casi el triple de cordobeses que la media, o sea que era un destino de esa migración. Que las dos familias vinieran de allí no es una señal de que se conocieran: es la demografía del pueblo.
  • LOS BUDIÑO ESTABAN EN EL MISMO PUEBLO Y VENÍAN DEL MISMO SITIO. Antoliano Budiño —el otro bisabuelo, el padre de Rosa— es la ficha 73360 de este mismo censo, y Evergisto Rivas la 73497: el mismo distrito de Helvecia, a menos de ciento cuarenta líneas de padrón. Y las dos familias son de CÓRDOBA. Veintidós años antes de que naciera Ramón Marcos, sus dos ramas paternas vivían en el mismo pueblo del Paraná.

Where he lived
HELVECIA, department of San José, in 1887 · department of LA CAPITAL in 1895. The schedule gives the department, not the district, and La Capital was large back then: it included CAYASTACITO, EMILIA and other country villages besides the city
Cayastacito and La Capital
CAYASTACITO WAS INSIDE THE DEPARTMENT OF LA CAPITAL in 1887 — the provincial census records it there, with 661 inhabitants — and by 1909 it appears in that of SAN JUSTO: the boundaries moved in between. So this family may have been at or near Cayastacito in 1895, which is where their grandson was born fourteen years later
Trade
ESTANCIERO (rancher), and owning real property, according to the 1895 national census
How many estancieros there were
In the 1887 provincial census there are 2,460 estancieros in Santa Fe: the SEVENTH most common occupation, behind farmer, day labourer, farmhand, ploughman, shopkeeper and seamstress. In the dependency column there are 16,249 «patrón» against 16,500 «peón». And his 1887 house held TWO family units, his widowed mother and an uncle, under one roof
His mother
LUISA SOSA DE BUDIÑO, a widow born in Córdoba, who lived with him in both censuses
Sabía leer y escribir

The Budiño household at Helvecia, 1887 provincial census

Helvecia, departamento de San José, Santa Fe, Argentina · book 8, line 10 onwards · 1887

Archive
Archivo General de la Provincia de Santa Fe · CFI
Colección
Primer Censo Provincial de Santa Fe, 1887 — padrones con información nominativa, buscador público y gratuito
Cómo se encontró
reconstruido por números de ficha correlativos: en este censo el índice sigue el orden de las líneas del padrón, así que pidiendo las fichas contiguas sale el hogar entero
Enlace
santafe.gov.ar/censo1887 — fichas 73497 a 73501 (Rivas) y 73360 a 73367 (Budiño)
Warning
la profesión y la religión salen «bloqueado» sin cuenta; el resto de los campos es público

Translation

The roll's records, in the order in which they are written:

· BUDINO ANTOLIANO, male, 33, HEAD, married, Argentine. · ÁLVARES TERESA, female, 18, WIFE, married, Argentine. Children borne: 0. Years married: 1. · SOSA LUISA DE BUDINO, female, 60, MOTHER, widow, born in CÓRDOBA. Children borne: 4. Years married: 30. · SOSA PEDRO, male, 60, UNCLE, widower. · BUDINO MANUEL, male, 34, head (of another unit in the same house), married. · BUDINO ROSARIO, male, 10, son · BUDINO JUANA, female, 6, daughter. · BUDINO ANASTACIO, male, 20, NEPHEW, single.

What you need to know about this record

  • THIS RECORD SETTLES A READING PROBLEM THAT HAD BEEN CARRIED FORWARD. In Ramón Marcos's birth record, the maternal grandmother's surname is written in a hand that read as «Leiva» or «Deira». The 1887 census registers her explicitly as Antoliano's WIFE and writes it ÁLVARES TERESA, and the 1895 national census repeats it. It is Álvarez, and my first reading was wrong.
  • ONE YEAR MARRIED AND NO CHILDREN IN 1887. They married in 1886, and that fits the four children of the 1895 census: the eldest, Irineo, was seven there, so born about 1888.
  • LUISA SOSA DE BUDIÑO, the mother, at SIXTY, with FOUR children and THIRTY years of marriage, is the oldest person in this branch: born about 1827 in CÓRDOBA and married about 1857. With her the Galician surname enters the tree by way of Córdoba, not directly from Galicia.

Antoliano Budiño's household in the department of La Capital

La Capital, Santa Fe, Argentina · census schedule, lines 7 to 15 · May 1895

Archive
Archivo General de la Nación (Argentina)
Colección
Segundo Censo Nacional de la República Argentina, 1895
Cómo se encontró
FamilySearch, grupo de imágenes 004159962, imagen 236 de 512 — hallado por Gonzalo el 31 de agosto de 2026
State of the evidence
IDENTIFICADO EL 3 DE SEPTIEMBRE DE 2026, cuando la partida de nacimiento de Ramón Marcos dio el nombre de los padres de Rosa

Translation

A Budiño household in the department of La Capital, lines 7 to 15 of the schedule. The columns are surname and forename, sex, years completed, marital status, nation, province if Argentine, occupation and whether they can read and write.

· BUDIÑO ANTOLIANO, male, 35, married, Argentine, born at SANTA FE, by trade an ESTANCIERO (rancher), owns real property. · BUDIÑO LUISA, female, 65, WIDOW, Argentine, born at CÓRDOBA. · ÁLVAREZ TERESA, female, 24, married, Argentine, Santa Fe. · BUDIÑO JUANA, 17, single, Argentine. · BUDIÑO ROSARIO, 20, single, Argentine, DAY LABOURER. · BUDIÑO IRINEO, 7 · BUDIÑO DELMIRO, 5 · BUDIÑO FERNANDO, 2 · BUDIÑO ROSA, 1.

What you need to know about this record

  • THE ONE-YEAR-OLD ROSA OF THIS SCHEDULE IS GONZALO'S GREAT-GRANDMOTHER. For a few days there were two candidates and neither was proven. It was closed by her son's birth record, which names her parents: ANTOLIANO BUDIÑO and TERESA ÁLVAREZ. They are the couple on lines 7 and 9 of this very schedule.
  • THE AGE DOES NOT QUITE MATCH AND IT IS ONLY FAIR TO SAY SO. Here she is ONE YEAR OLD in May 1895, so born about 1894; in December 1909 she declares SEVENTEEN, so born about 1892. Two years' difference. In these rolls the ages of small children are notoriously loose, and a sixteen-year-old unmarried mother declaring seventeen would surprise nobody either. The identification does not rest on the age but on the parents' names, which are unmistakable.
  • THE SCHEDULE GIVES THE DEPARTMENT, NOT THE DISTRICT: «La Capital». And in those years La Capital included CAYASTACITO — 661 inhabitants in the 1887 census — which only later passed to the department of San Justo. So this household may have been at or near Cayastacito rather than in the city.
  • The same household appears eight years earlier at HELVECIA, in the 1887 provincial census: Antoliano aged 33, Teresa aged 18 newly married, the mother Luisa born in Córdoba, Rosario and Juana. The family moved from Helvecia to the capital between 1887 and 1895.

How the surname travelled
BUDIÑO IS GALICIAN, from Pontevedra and Ourense, but the Budiños of Santa Fe are almost all born in CÓRDOBA or children of people from Córdoba. The route was Galicia → Córdoba → Santa Fe, and she is the Córdoba step
Still alive in
mayo de 1895 — el censo nacional la encuentra con sesenta y cinco años, viviendo en casa de su hijo Antoliano
Death
not located — later than May 1895, when she was still alive

No record obtained yet

She appears only in two censuses, and in both as the widowed mother living in her son's house. What those two lines give is more than it looks: born in Córdoba about 1827, married about 1857, four children, widowed before 1887. Her birth and her marriage would be in the parish books of CÓRDOBA — a whole province this genealogy has not yet touched.

Ancestor

Ramón Rivas

Gonzalo's great-grandfather. Throughout this investigation he was «the man who erased himself»: a name with no dates, no origin and no face. On 3 September 2026 his death record gave him back whole. He was born at SAN JUSTO on 14 January 1889, into a family come from Córdoba; he was a HACENDADO (landowner) all his life; he had Ramón Marcos with Rosa Budiño at the age of twenty without marrying her, and acknowledged him formally thirty-three years later; he married Cristina Romero and outlived her; and he died at San Justo on 24 August 1971, aged eighty-two.

Child of Evergisto Rivas · Rita Álvarez

Married Rosa Budiño

Parent of Ramón Marcos Rivas · Brother of Marcos Ramón

Confirmed by record
YES, and now by three: his son's marriage (Jobson, 1948, act 15), his son's death record (Santa Fe, 2002) and, since 3 September 2026, HIS OWN DEATH RECORD (San Justo, 1971, act 84)
Birth
14 JANUARY 1889, at SAN JUSTO, province of Santa Fe
Death
24 AUGUST 1971, at ten in the morning, at SAN JUSTO, of illness, certified by Dr Edgardo Nelson García. Eighty-two years old
Nationality
ARGENTINE — not an immigrant, contrary to what might have been assumed
Trade
HACENDADO (landowner), and he remained one to the end: it is stated so in his son's 1948 record and again in his own death record of 1971, twenty-three years later
Identity document
L.E. 2,356,204

EVERGISTO RIVAS and RITA ÁLVAREZ, both born in CÓRDOBA. This is the whole line that was missing, and it was found afterwards in the 1887 census living at HELVECIA
Spouse
CRISTINA ROMERO. In 1971 he is recorded as a WIDOWER, so she died first. She was not Ramón Marcos's mother
Earlier known partner
ROSA BUDIÑO, mother of Ramón Marcos and already dead by 1948. THERE IS NO RECORD THAT THEY MARRIED: no document links them except through the son
Children
RAMÓN MARCOS (Cayastacito, 28 November 1909) and at least one brother of his, unnamed, whom the family remembers. By several women, so the real number is unknown
Did he acknowledge his son
YES, AND WITH A DATE: 3 September 1942, by act 201 of the birth book of the San Justo office. It is noted in the margin of Ramón Marcos's birth record. He was fifty-three; his son, thirty-two
How old he was when he became a father
TWENTY. He was born in January 1889 and Ramón Marcos in November 1909. Rosa Budiño was seventeen
How many Rivas there were
MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED. In the 1887 provincial census of Santa Fe there are SIXTY-EIGHT people surnamed Rivas in the whole province. They were listed one by one to find his family, and it turned up: the Helvecia household
Why he was not in San Justo in 1887
BECAUSE THEY HAD NOT ARRIVED YET. It had been measured that there were NO Rivas at all in the department of San Justo in 1887, out of 1,655 people indexed, and he was born there in January 1889. The family was at HELVECIA, the next department, and moved between the two dates
Family memory, checked
Gonzalo remembers his family always at VERA, SAN JUSTO, CAYASTÁ and CAYASTACITO. Helvecia is in that same Paraná corridor, south of Cayastá

his own death record, filed here; and the two records of his son, in his son's profile

San Justo, departamento de San Justo, Santa Fe, Argentina · death entry no. 84 · copy 117863 · 24 August 1971, at 10 a.m.; registered on the 25th

Miguel [—] Nelli, jefe del Registro Civil de San Justo
Qué se archiva aquí
sólo la hoja del acta. La segunda hoja del PDF original es el comprobante de pago y lleva datos personales de Gonzalo: no se archiva ni se publica

Translation

DEATH ENTRY No. 84.

At SAN JUSTO, department of SAN JUSTO, province of Santa Fe, Argentine Republic, on the TWENTY-FIFTH day of AUGUST 1971, before me, head of the Registry of Civil Status, there appears ISMAEL OLIVA, identity document 2,510,056, resident at San Justo, declaring that:

At TEN HOURS on the TWENTY-FOURTH day of the current month, and at SAN JUSTO, there died RAMÓN RIVAS, male, of ARGENTINE nationality, a WIDOWER, by profession a HACENDADO (landowner), resident at SAN JUSTO, identity document L.E. 2,356,204, BORN AT SAN JUSTO ON 14 JANUARY 1889, as a consequence of ILLNESS, according to the medical certificate of Dr EDGARDO NELSON GARCÍA.

That he was married to CRISTINA ROMERO. SON OF EVERGISTO RIVAS AND RITA ÁLVAREZ.

Read out, the declarant signs together with me.

What you need to know about this record

  • THIS IS THE DOCUMENT THAT GIVES BACK THE MAN WHO HAD ERASED HIMSELF. Until 3 September 2026, all that was known of Ramón Rivas was what his son's records said: Argentine, of San Justo, a landowner, and alive in April 1948. Not one date. This record gives his exact birth, his exact death, his identity number, his wife and his parents, in fourteen lines.
  • HE WAS BORN ON 14 JANUARY 1889, SO HE WAS TWENTY WHEN HE BECAME A FATHER. And that rules out at a stroke the two candidates that had been under consideration: the four-year-old of the Rosario census (born about 1891) and the twelve-year-old of the San Javier census (born about 1875). He was neither.
  • IT GIVES TWO NEW GENERATIONS IN A SINGLE LINE: «SON OF EVERGISTO RIVAS AND RITA ÁLVAREZ». With those two names the 1887 provincial census was searched again and both appeared, together, as head and wife of a household at HELVECIA — with three daughters and seven years of marriage. They have profiles of their own.
  • HE DIED A LANDOWNER AT EIGHTY-TWO. The same trade he declared in 1948 at his son's wedding. Twenty-three years later he was still the same thing, which says it was not a run of luck but a position.
  • HE WAS THE WIDOWER OF CRISTINA ROMERO, WHO IS NOT RAMÓN MARCOS'S MOTHER. Rosa Budiño had died before April 1948 and was never married to him. Cristina Romero is a new woman in this story and had also died before 1971.
  • AN ODDITY THAT RESOLVES ITSELF: it had been measured that in 1887 there was NOT ONE Rivas in the department of San Justo, out of 1,655 people indexed, and here he is born there in January 1889. The answer is in the census: in 1887 the family lived at HELVECIA, department of San José, next door. They moved between the census and the birth.
  • WHO DECLARED IT: ISMAEL OLIVA, of San Justo, document 2,510,056. Not family, by the surname. In 1971 Ramón Marcos was living in the city of Santa Fe and was sixty-one; that the errand fell to a neighbour and not to the son fits a distant relationship, which is exactly what the family remembers.
Sibling

Brother of Marcos Ramón

He came to visit Marcos Ramón when both were already old men. He looked exactly like him. We do not know his name.

Child of Ramón Rivas

Father
Ramón Rivas
What is remembered
that he turned up on a visit when they were both already old and that the likeness between the two was complete
Source
family memory

No record obtained yet

No name, and that is precisely the fact that would yield most: with his name and his record the father's full name is fixed at a stroke. It can be asked of Marcos César and his brothers, who lived through that visit.

Ancestor

Tebaldo Celeri

In the records: TEBALDO JACOPO ARCHIMEDE CELERI on his birth record

Gonzalo's paternal great-grandfather. He emigrated from Italy and ended up in the north of Santa Fe province, at Jobson, beside Vera station. He lost his first wife and married again in 1900, aged thirty-eight, to a girl of nineteen. For months he was a nameless gap in this tree: «the Celeri immigrant». On 27 August 2026 he stopped being one.

Child of Callisto Celeri · Enrichetta Vanni

Parent of Elva Agustina Celeri · Enriqueta Celeri · Olga Celeri · Ercilia Celeri · Rosa Celeri · Nerina Celeri

Birth
25 JULY 1862, at eight in the morning, at TRASSILICO (Garfagnana, province of Massa, today of Lucca) — record 52
Full name
TEBALDO JACOPO ARCHIMEDE. Three names, and two of them learned ones: Jacopo and ARCHIMEDE, after Archimedes. It is not a peasant's name
Where he was born
in the house of the signora LUISA VANNI, at the place in Trassilico called ALLA MAESTÀ. The house of his mother's family, not his father's
Baptised
on the very day of his birth, by don DOMENICO GRANDI, parish priest of Trassilico
Parents
CALLISTO CELERI, aged 31, TOWN CLERK of Trassilico, son of the late DESIDERIO; and ENRICHETTA VANNI, aged 27, daughter of the late DOCTOR PIETRO VANNI
First wife
ENCARNACIÓN FERNÁNDEZ. By October 1900 he was already a WIDOWER of hers
Second wife
MARGARITA ALASIA, aged nineteen, a native of Colonia Emilia — married at VERA (Santa Fe) on 25 October 1900
Address
JOBSON, Vera station, department of Vera, in the north of the province of Santa Fe. A district of farming colonies founded by immigrants
Known children
from the first marriage, ENRIQUETA (c. 1894). From the second, ELBA and her sisters Olga, Ercilia, Rosa and Nerina
Origin
TRASSILICO, province of LUCCA, in the Tuscan Garfagnana — CONFIRMED on 27 August 2026. The two Argentine records said only «natural de Italia»; what fixes it is the death record of his MOTHER, who died at Trassilico in 1923. Family memory had the exact village right
The family house
TRASSILICO, VIA DEL CENTRO, NUMBER 17 — the address where his mother died, and the place he set out from for Argentina. ver en el mapa
The street today
VIA DEL CENTRO NO LONGER EXISTS. OpenStreetMap was asked for every named street in Trassilico and there are eleven, none of that name: Via Antonio Vallisneri, Via San Ansano, Via Leonardo Nobli, Via della Quercia, Via di Mezzo, Via Borghetto, Via Caraglia delle Gioie, Via della Fontana, and the squares Umberto I, Giovanni Pierelli and Guglielmo Marconi. The names changed.
Probable street
VIA ANTONIO VALLISNERI. All eleven streets measured one by one: Via della Fontana is 2,085 m long but it is the access road up from the valley, outside the built-up village. Within the village the longest are San Ansano (362 m) and VIA ANTONIO VALLISNERI (335 m) — and the centre of Vallisneri falls at 44.04151, 10.40348, which is exactly the centroid of the village. IT IS THE CENTRAL STREET. And ANTONIO VALLISNERI (1661-1730), the famous naturalist, WAS BORN AT TRASSILICO: he is the village's illustrious son. ver en el mapa
Why it changed
TRASSILICO WAS A COMUNE OF ITS OWN until Gallicano absorbed it, around 1928. When two comuni merge, streets with duplicate names have to be renamed, and «Via del Centro» is just the kind of generic name that collides — Gallicano would have had its own. The natural thing is that the Trassilico one should be renamed after the illustrious son. And it fits the dates: Enrichetta died at Via del Centro 17 in 1923, FIVE YEARS BEFORE the merger. That was the name in use then. ALL THIS IS INFERENCE, not document.
Alternative
There is a number 17 actually mapped, in VIA LEONARDO NOBLI (44.04216, 10.40396), beside the Rocca Estense. But that is the street at the northern end, not the centre. Vallisneri, on the other hand, has only the EVEN door numbers mapped, from 16 to 32: nobody has mapped the odd ones, so a number 17 there is perfectly possible and simply does not appear.
How to prove it
The STRADARIO of the Comune di Gallicano, which usually preserves the concordance between old and new names. It is a two-line question by post: comunegallicano@postacert.toscana.it
The church
SS. PIETRO E PAOLO of Trassilico — where he was baptised on the very day of his birth and where his parents had married three years earlier. It is the building that has been located. ver en el mapa

What the manuscript says

52. L'anno 1862 milleottocento sessantadue, il giorno 30 trenta del mese di Luglio. Davanti me sottoscritto BERTOLI BARSOTTI RANIERI, Sindaco ufficiale dello Stato Civile del Comune di TRASSILICO, Circondario di GARFAGNANA, Provincia di MASSA, è comparso CELERI CALLISTO FU DESIDERIO, di anni 31 trentuno, SEGRETARIO COMUNALE, il quale, presenti Andreini Michele fu Matteo di anni 60 sessanta e Andreini Pietro fu Carlo di anni 30, ambidue DOTTORI FISICI e domiciliati tutti in Trassilico, ha dichiarato che nel giorno 25 venticinque del mese ed anno suddetti, alle ore 8 otto antemeridiane, gli è nato in casa della Signora VANNI LUISA posta in Trassilico luogo detto ALLA MAESTÀ, da CELERI ENRICHETTA DEL FU VANNI DOTTOR PIETRO di anni 27 ventisette sua consorte, un figlio di sesso mascolino, cui furono imposti i nomi di TEBALDO JACOPO ARCHIMEDE, e come dalla fede del Battesimo conferitogli dal Signor Don DOMENICO GRANDI Parroco locale il giorno stesso della sua nascita, che si unisce.

Translation

Record 52. The year 1862, on the THIRTIETH OF JULY. Before me, RANIERI BERTOLI BARSOTTI, mayor and registrar of the Civil Status of the COMUNE OF TRASSILICO, district of the GARFAGNANA, province of MASSA, there appeared:

CALLISTO CELERI, SON OF THE LATE DESIDERIO, aged THIRTY-ONE, TOWN CLERK.

Who, in the presence of MICHELE ANDREINI, son of the late Matteo, aged sixty, and PIETRO ANDREINI, son of the late Carlo, aged thirty, BOTH PHYSICIANS and all three resident at Trassilico, declared that on the TWENTY-FIFTH of the same month and year, AT EIGHT IN THE MORNING, there was born to him — in the house of signora LUISA VANNI, situated at Trassilico, in the place called ALLA MAESTÀ — of ENRICHETTA CELERI, DAUGHTER OF THE LATE DOCTOR PIETRO VANNI, aged TWENTY-SEVEN, his wife, a son of the male sex, upon whom were bestowed the names TEBALDO JACOPO ARCHIMEDE.

And so it appears in the certificate of baptism conferred on him by don DOMENICO GRANDI, the local parish priest, ON THE VERY DAY OF HIS BIRTH, which is annexed.

What you need to know about this record

  • IT IS THE RECORD THAT CLOSES THE CELERI BRANCH, AND IT APPEARED ON 27 AUGUST 2026, THE SAME DAY AS THE VILLAGE. Forty-eight hours earlier, Elba's father was «an Italian immigrant, from the north I think», without a name.
  • THE FAMILY WERE NOT COUNTRY PEOPLE, AND THIS CHANGES THE WHOLE PORTRAIT. The father was SEGRETARIO COMUNALE — the town clerk of Trassilico, the man who wrote the village's records. The maternal grandfather was a DOCTOR OF MEDICINE. Both witnesses were PHYSICIANS. And the child was named ARCHIMEDE, after Archimedes. Compare this with the Calabrian branch, where all of them, without exception, are braccianti, pecorai and contadini.
  • A NEW GENERATION: DESIDERIO CELERI. «Celeri Callisto FU DESIDERIO» — Callisto's father, already dead in 1862. With Callisto aged 31 in 1862, Desiderio would be of the generation of 1800 or earlier.
  • THE MOTHER'S AGE FITS TO THE LETTER. Here she is 27 in July 1862 → born about 1835. Her death record of 1923 gives her 88 → born about 1835. Two documents separated by sixty-one years and by a thousand kilometres of archive, with the same figure.
  • PIETRO VANNI WAS NOT A PHYSICIAN BUT A LAWYER, and this must be corrected: from this record it was inferred that «dottor Pietro» meant a doctor of medicine, and it did not. The marriage record of his other daughter, Luisa, in April 1863, says so unambiguously: «di professione LEGALE, nato in LUCCA». He was a man of law and he came from the city. He had already died by 1862.
  • HE WAS BORN IN THE HOUSE OF SIGNORA LUISA VANNI, at the place called ALLA MAESTÀ — and on 27 August 2026 it became known who she was: HIS MOTHER'S ELDER SISTER. Luisa's marriage record, of April 1863, declares her the daughter of the same parents. Tebaldo was born in the house of his own aunt: Enrichetta went to give birth at her sister's. Sixty-one years later his mother would die in the VIA DEL CENTRO, number 17. They are two different addresses in the same village.
  • «TRASSILICO, CIRCONDARIO DI GARFAGNANA, PROVINCIA DI MASSA». In 1862 the Garfagnana belonged to the province of MASSA, not to that of Lucca. That is why its registers are in the Archivio di Stato di Massa and not in that of Lucca — a fact that took some finding and is best not forgotten.
